| 2026-05-10 | **Brief 97 completed:** Forms feature — submission webhook + daily R2 cleanup cron. Ninth user-visible deliverable for the form-builder feature; with this brief landed `splash-forms` has its complete runtime — Power Automate gets pinged on every successful submission (when bound), and orphan R2 cleanup happens daily without operator intervention. Webhook posture mirrors damage-worker's `CUSTOMER_CLAIM_WEBHOOK_URL` (Brief 32 / 48): worker-level secret `FORMS_SUBMISSION_WEBHOOK_URL` (optional, fail-soft when unbound — `wrangler tail` logs `[forms.webhook] fire failed (fail-soft)` on error or `[forms.webhook] non-2xx response: <status>` on a non-2xx PA response). Per-form opt-out via `forms.notify_webhook = false`. 15s `AbortController` timeout. **Files-by-URL** in payload (NOT base64 — planning Decision 6 confirmation): each file/signature entry carries `download_url` pointing at `/forms/admin/api/files/{r2_key}` (Brief 92 admin-gated serve route). PA fetches the URL when needed and authenticates as a service account. This dodges the >3MB base64 cap pain that hit damage-worker's customer-claim webhook. `inferAdminBase` helper rewrites `*.workers.dev` request origins to `https://splashcarwashes.info` for the `splash_admin_url` link in the payload — operators click these from email, and staging/workers.dev URLs would 404 for recipients; staging hostname (`staging.splashcarwashes.info`) passes through unchanged. **Webhook is SKIPPED on idempotent re-submits** (`!inserted.wasNew`) so a network retry never delivers duplicate PA notifications. Fired via `ctx.waitUntil` after the canonical submission insert lands — does NOT block the success page response. The `handleSubmit` signature was extended end-to-end with `ctx: ExecutionContext`: the worker fetch handler's `_ctx` was un-prefixed and plumbed through to the submit route's call site. Daily R2 cleanup cron at **11:00 UTC** (`[triggers] crons = ["0 11 * * *"]` in `apps/forms-worker/wrangler.toml`) — picked to NOT collide with damage-worker's daily summary at 13:00 UTC (Brief 65) or workorders-worker's MaintainX sync at 11:30 UTC (Brief 71). Two passes inside `runDailyCleanup`, both fail-soft. **Pass 1 (orphan submission files)** lists R2 under `form-submission-files/`, groups objects by `pending_submission_id` extracted from the path (`form-submission-files/{form_id}/{pending_submission_id}/{field_key}/{filename}` per Brief 92 conventions), queries Supabase `form_submissions.id IN (...)`, deletes R2 objects with no matching row AND `uploaded > cutoff` (24h grace covers the gap between out-of-band uploads from Brief 92 and the eventual submit POST that creates the row). Hard pagination cap 50 pages × 1000 = 50K objects per run; pagination cap hit surfaces a soft error to the cron's return value but does not abort. **Pass 2 (orphan form assets)** lists R2 under `form-assets/`, queries `form_assets.r2_key IN (...)`, deletes R2 objects with no matching row AND `uploaded > 1h ago` — conservative grace against the (currently impossible, defense-in-depth) race where an asset's R2 put has landed but its row insert hasn't. Hard pagination cap 20 pages × 1000 = 20K objects. `runDailyCleanup` returns `{submissionFilesDeleted, assetsDeleted, submissionPagesScanned, assetPagesScanned, errors}` and logs `[forms.cleanup] complete` with all five fields on every run; the scheduled handler additionally logs `[forms.cleanup.cron] result`. The `[observability.logs]` block from Brief 89 covers scheduled invocations automatically (eventType: scheduled in CF dashboard). No new env vars beyond the optional `FORMS_SUBMISSION_WEBHOOK_URL` (recommended for production); no new bindings. Files created: `apps/forms-worker/src/submit/webhook.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/cron/cleanup.ts`. Files modified: `apps/forms-worker/src/submit/index.ts` (handleSubmit signature gains `ctx`; webhook fire wrapped in `ctx.waitUntil`; `inserted` declaration's row type widened from `{id: string}` to `SubmissionRow` so the helper's actual return type is exposed for the webhook payload's `submitterKind` / `submitterEmail` / `submittedAt` field reads), `apps/forms-worker/src/index.ts` (default export now `{fetch, scheduled}`; submit route plumbs `ctx` through), `apps/forms-worker/wrangler.toml` (`[triggers] crons = ["0 11 * * *"]` block added; comment block updated), `PRE_DEPLOY_FORMS.md` (10 Brief-97 smoke tests added in Section 5), `CLAUDE.md` (forms-worker glossary extended with the Brief 97 paragraph), `BUILD_STATE.md`, `BRIEFS/INDEX.md`, `BRIEFS/QUEUE.md`. Decisions made on operator's behalf: (1) Brief's sample code imported `createServiceClient` from `../db/forms`, but `db/forms.ts` doesn't export it — it uses direct PostgREST `fetch()` (Brief 89/94 pattern). I imported `createServiceClient` from `@splash/db-supabase` instead, matching the existing pattern in `submit/index.ts`'s lookup path. (2) `inferAdminBase` rewrites both `*.workers.dev` AND defaults staging hostnames to pass through; for `splashcarwashes.info` (production same-origin) it also passes through. The brief's sample only handled `workers.dev`; I extended to make staging explicit too — the resulting behavior is identical but the intent is clearer. (3) Skipped firing the webhook on idempotent re-submits — the brief's sample wires `ctx.waitUntil` unconditionally after the file rows insert, but `inserted.wasNew === false` means the canonical row already exists from an earlier successful POST that DID fire the webhook. Re-firing would deliver duplicate PA notifications. The Brief 91 docblock on `insertSubmissionIdempotent` flagged this as a "Brief 97 may use this to gate webhook fires" intention, so I followed through. (4) Used `ScheduledController` (not `ScheduledEvent`) in the scheduled handler signature, matching damage-worker / workorders-worker — the brief's sample used `ScheduledEvent` which is the older type name. (5) Returned `submissionPagesScanned` / `assetPagesScanned` from `runDailyCleanup` so the operator can observe per-run R2 list pagination cost without grepping logs — minor extension over the brief's `{submissionFilesDeleted, assetsDeleted, errors}` shape. Latent issues: (a) **R2 list pagination cost.** Each `env.FORMS_FILES.list({limit:1000})` is one R2 op. At current bucket size (effectively empty pre-deploy), the cron will be 1 page per pass = 2 R2 list ops + 0 deletes per run; cost is negligible. Will scale linearly with bucket size; if either pagination cap (50 pages submission / 20 pages assets) starts getting hit regularly, frequency can be increased without code change (just edit `[triggers] crons`) or the per-run cap can be raised. (b) **Webhook payload size.** PA flow with files-by-URL keeps the payload bounded — submission row + file refs only, no embedded bytes. No size cap needed at v1. If a future field type embeds large derived data in the payload JSONB (e.g., signature SVG strings expanded into the payload), a size cap on the webhook body would become prudent. (c) **Webhook does not surface DLQ / retry.** Per CLAUDE.md / damage-worker precedent, fail-soft means a transient PA outage drops notifications. PA itself can replay from a queue if it builds one; v1 worker does not. Out of scope per the brief: per-form custom webhook URLs (Decision 6 — security deferral), webhook payload base64-when-small flavor (Decision 6 — picked URL-only), webhook retry / DLQ (fail-soft per damage-worker precedent), cron frequency tuning (daily is the agreed cadence), emailing operators on cleanup errors (logs only v1). Validation: `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-worker typecheck` green, `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-worker exec wrangler deploy --dry-run` green (worker bundle 1043.10 KiB / 199.91 KiB gzip — no significant size delta vs Brief 96), root `pnpm typecheck` green across all 17 packages. Cron testing deferred to operator (manual trigger from CF dashboard's Cron Triggers UI); first scheduled run will fire at the next 11:00 UTC. | Claude Code |

| 2026-05-10 | **Brief 94 completed:** Forms feature — admin API (CRUD + draft/publish lifecycle + assets + lookup-sources). Sixth user-visible deliverable for the form-builder feature; `splash-forms` now has its complete admin-side API surface. **Scope.** New `apps/forms-worker/src/admin/{auth,forms,assets,lookup-sources}.ts` exposes 9 admin routes under `/forms/admin/api/*` (list / create / get-detail / save-draft / publish / unpublish / republish / asset-upload / asset-delete / lookup-sources). New `apps/forms-worker/src/db/admin-forms.ts` is the worker-side DB layer, using direct PostgREST `fetch()` (matches the Brief 89 pattern in `db/forms.ts`). Auth gate mirrors fleet's Brief 83: `session.role === "super_admin"` OR `session.dcRole === "admin"|"super_admin"`. Per-location scoping deferred to v2 (planning Decision 7). 503 returned uniformly when `SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY` is unbound; CSRF defense via `isOriginAllowed` on every POST/PATCH/DELETE. Lifecycle (B-classic, planning Decision 1): draft (mutable) → published (immutable, current_version_id pinned) → archived (no public render, submissions retained). Publish (`apps/forms-worker/src/db/admin-forms.ts:publishDraft`) creates a new immutable version row AND spawns a fresh editable draft (clone of just-published) — operator can immediately start editing v(N+1) without losing v(N) as the public-facing version. PATCH /draft validates the schema body via `formSchemaSchema.safeParse` from `@splash/forms-schema` — same Zod boundary that protects the public render path in `db/forms.ts:getCurrentVersion`. Asset upload (`apps/forms-worker/src/admin/assets.ts:handleAssetUpload`) MIME-sniffs via `file-type` (first ~4 KB), allows JPEG/PNG/GIF/WebP only (no SVG XSS, no PDF), 10 MB hard cap (lower than the 25 MB submission-file cap because in-form assets ship to every form view), R2 path `form-assets/{form_id}/{asset_id}.{ext}`; row-insert failure triggers best-effort R2 rollback (cron from Brief 97 is the safety net). New `apps/web/app/admin/forms/_lib/worker-fetch.ts` mirrors fleet's Brief 83/87 helper — service-binding-first (`FORMS_WORKER`), URL-fallback for `next dev` — exposes typed wrappers for every Brief 94 endpoint plus shared response interfaces. `apps/web/package.json` gains `@splash/forms-schema` workspace dep so the helper can re-export `FormMeta` / `FormSchema` types Brief 95's builder UI will consume. No Delete endpoint per planning Decision 7 — destructive ops via SQL only; CASCADE FKs from Brief 89 make `DELETE FROM forms WHERE id=...` safe. **Decisions made on operator's behalf:** (a) `authenticate(env, cookieHeader)` from the brief's stub doesn't exist — actual signature is `authenticate(req, env)` returning `{status, session?}`; used the canonical surface (matches `apps/forms-worker/src/uploads/serve.ts:24` and `apps/fleet-inquiry-worker/src/admin.js:148`). (b) DB layer uses direct PostgREST `fetch()` not `createServiceClient` — matches the existing `db/forms.ts` pattern; the brief's sketch imported `createServiceClient` from `./forms` but that helper isn't exported there. (c) `listForms` uses PostgREST FK column hint syntax `form_versions!form_id(count)` to disambiguate the embedded count — without the hint PostgREST 400s on "multiple relationships" (form_versions has both back-ref `form_id` AND forward-refs from `forms.current_version_id` / `forms.draft_version_id`). (d) `lastPublishedAt` resolved via a follow-up call against `form_versions?id=in.(<current_version_ids>)` rather than a deeper embed — keeps the embed expression readable. (e) Atomicity: 3-call create + 4-call publish are sequential PostgREST writes (no transaction wrapper); rely on Brief 89's DEFERRABLE FKs from forms → form_versions for partial-failure containment. Acceptable for v1 — partial-failure recovery is via SQL only, flagged on the next admin GET (orphan form NULL draft_version_id, or dangling current_version_id pointing at a non-draft with no new draft spawned). (f) Slug regex `^[a-z0-9][a-z0-9-]{2,80}$` (3–81 chars, lowercase alphanum + hyphen, leading non-hyphen). (g) `description` null-coalesced when blank — JSONB column allows NULL; treating empty string as null keeps detail-page display logic simpler. (h) Asset upload accepts any form_id without cross-checking the form exists; DELETE rejects on form_mismatch and R2 rollback covers row-insert failures, with cron sweeping orphans. (i) `apps/web/package.json` gains `@splash/forms-schema` as a runtime dep (workspace ref) — Brief 95's builder UI will consume the full type contract; adding here saves Brief 95 a pnpm-install round-trip. (j) Admin endpoints emit `Cache-Control: no-store` on list + detail (admin views must reflect just-published edits immediately); lookup-sources caches `private, max-age=300`. (k) Apps/web helper returns `null` on 401/403/404 (same shape as fleet's helper) so server components branch cleanly without try/catch on auth failures. **Latent issues found:** (1) `noUncheckedIndexedAccess: true` requires `versions[0]?.count ?? 0` rather than `versions[0].count` in `listForms`. (2) PostgREST embedded count + FK disambiguation depends on the FK relationship metadata existing in Postgres — Brief 89's schema has the explicit `forms_current_version_fk` constraint plus the implicit form_versions/form_submissions FKs, so embedding works. (3) Image dimension capture (`width` / `height` on `form_assets`) deferred to Brief 95's client-side `<img>` probe before upload — extracting from worker code requires a binary image parser (heavy). Inserted as `null` for now. (4) The brief's draft sketch had inaccuracies (authenticate signature, createServiceClient import path) flagged in the Outcome.atomic-create note. **Files created:** `apps/forms-worker/src/admin/{auth,forms,assets,lookup-sources}.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/db/admin-forms.ts`, `apps/web/app/admin/forms/_lib/worker-fetch.ts`. **Files modified:** `apps/forms-worker/src/index.ts` (9 new route matchers + 9 new imports + header docblock extended), `apps/web/package.json` (new `@splash/forms-schema: workspace:*`), `pnpm-lock.yaml` (relink), `PRE_DEPLOY_FORMS.md` (Section 5 gains 15 Brief-94 smoke tests), `CLAUDE.md` (forms-worker glossary Brief 94 paragraph), `BUILD_STATE.md` (this entry + Last-updated bump + prioritized work list row 94), `BRIEFS/INDEX.md` (Brief 94 row), `BRIEFS/QUEUE.md` (Brief 94 marked completed), `BRIEFS/brief-094-forms-admin-api-crud.md` (Status → Completed; Outcome filled). **Validation:** `pnpm install` ran (relinked `@splash/forms-schema` into apps/web/node_modules); `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-schema typecheck` green; `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-worker typecheck` green; `pnpm --filter @splash/web typecheck` green; `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-worker exec wrangler deploy --dry-run` green; root `pnpm typecheck` green. | 2026-05-09 | **Brief 92 completed:** Forms feature — file + signature uploads, R2-backed. Third user-visible deliverable for the form-builder feature; with this brief landed every field type except `lookup` (Brief 93) is functional end-to-end. **Scope.** New `apps/forms-worker/src/limits.ts` (HARD_LIMITS: 25 MB per file, 100 MB per submission, 20 files per submission, 1 MB per signature, 24h pending TTL — non-overridable from field config; DEFAULT_LIMITS: 10 MB per-field default, image/* + application/pdf default MIME allow-list). New `apps/forms-worker/src/uploads/{file,signature,serve,static}.ts`: file (`POST /forms/api/upload/{slug}` — multipart parse, MIME-sniff via `file-type@^19.6.0` reading first ~4 KB, per-field cap clamping to HARD_LIMITS, R2 put under `form-submission-files/{form_id}/{pending_submission_id}/{field_key}/{filename}` with `originalFilename` + `pendingSubmissionId` + `formId` + `fieldKey` customMetadata for cron filtering); signature (`POST /forms/api/signature/{slug}` — same shape, MIME derived from `field.format` not request, returns `{r2_key, format, size_bytes}`); serve (`GET /forms/admin/api/files/{r2_key}` — `@splash/auth authenticate()` gate matching Brief 83 fleet posture: super_admin role OR admin/super_admin dcRole; inline-display for images, force-download with `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="..."` from R2 customMetadata for everything else); static (vendored `signature_pad@4.2.0` + `forms-public.js` served from `/forms/api/static/{filename}` with `public, max-age=86400`). Vendored `apps/forms-worker/static/signature-pad.min.js` (11479 bytes, SHA-256 `49050fd4c2a4c66eff11a54f2552af743bb0681cde745760667c61e9c690b3e0`, MIT, fetched from `unpkg.com/signature_pad@4/dist/signature_pad.umd.min.js` — committed, never CDN-loaded at runtime per CLAUDE.md supply-chain posture). New `apps/forms-worker/static/forms-public.js` (~190 LOC vanilla JS, no framework) wires per-form: signature canvases via `SignaturePad` with 800ms debounced `endStroke` upload + clear-button handler, file inputs with `change`-event upload + dynamically-created hidden `${key}_r2` companion input, per-field error/status divs. Wrangler bundling: new `[[rules]] type = "Text" globs = ["**/static/*.js"] fallthrough = false` block — first dry-run failed with `static/*.js` (executor's first attempt mirroring the brief's sample) surfacing "module variable in ESM" parser warning + "no matching export 'default'" error because the implicit ESM rule for `.js` files matched first; switched to `**/static/*.js` and the rule matched correctly. New `apps/forms-worker/src/static-assets.d.ts` declares `*.js` text-import default-export shims so TypeScript resolves both imports as strings rather than failing on "module not found". `packages/forms-schema/src/validators/payload.ts` un-`null`s the file + signature validator branches: file → `z.object({r2_key: regex(/^form-submission-files\//), mime: z.string().min(1), size_bytes: z.number().int().positive(), original_filename: z.string().nullable()})`, signature → `z.object({r2_key: regex(...), format: z.enum(["png","svg"])})`; both `nullable().optional()` when `required === false`. `apps/forms-worker/src/submit/parse.ts` un-skips both: file reads `${field.key}_r2` hidden, signature reads `field.key` hidden, both produce `{r2_key, ...}` objects (or `null` when required+missing for the validator to flag). `apps/forms-worker/src/submit/index.ts` gains an R2-resolution pass between parse and validate: validate the r2_key starts with `form-submission-files/{form_id}/{pending_submission_id}/{field_key}/` (cross-submission key reuse → 400 `bad_r2_key`), HEAD the R2 object (missing → 400 `missing_file`), accumulate per-submission size + count (exceed → 413 `too_many_files` / `submission_too_large`), enrich the payload object with authoritative `head.size` / `head.httpMetadata.contentType` / `head.customMetadata.originalFilename`, queue a `form_submission_files` insert. `apps/forms-worker/src/db/forms.ts` extended with `insertSubmissionFiles(env, rows)` — best-effort PostgREST `Prefer: return=minimal` insert that runs AFTER the canonical submission row lands and only on `inserted.wasNew` (avoids double-insert on idempotent retries). `apps/forms-worker/src/render/shell.ts` injects `<script src="/forms/api/static/signature-pad.min.js" defer>` + `<script src="/forms/api/static/forms-public.js" defer>` in `<head>` (signature-pad must load first — defer preserves script tag order). Worker router gains four new path matchers (file upload / signature upload / static asset / admin file serve). **Decisions made on operator's behalf.** (a) Brief draft referenced `validateSession` from `@splash/auth` but the actual exported entry point is `authenticate(req, env)` returning `{status, session?}` — used the canonical surface in serve.ts. (b) `insertSubmissionFiles` uses direct PostgREST `fetch()` matching the existing forms-worker pattern (Brief 90/91), NOT `createServiceClient` from the brief's draft — `@supabase/supabase-js` isn't a forms-worker direct dep. (c) Wrangler `[[rules]]` glob uses `**/static/*.js`, not the brief's `static/*.js` — first dry-run failed because the implicit ESM rule for `.js` files matched first; the `**/` prefix is required for the Text rule to win. (d) `signature_pad@4.2.0` is the latest 4.x at fetch time (MIT, 11479 bytes) — operator can verify SHA-256 against unpkg's published hash. (e) Static asset URL uses `Cache-Control: public, max-age=86400` (24h) + `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff` — standard pattern for vendored assets that change only at deploy time. (f) `r2_key` validation pattern in submit handler matches Brief 90's renderer: each upload's r2_key must be prefixed with `form-submission-files/{form_id}/{pending_submission_id}/{field_key}/` so a malicious client can't reference another submission's file at submit time. (g) `form_submission_files` insert is best-effort (failure logged but doesn't reverse the canonical submission insert) — relies on Brief 97's orphan cleanup to sweep R2 objects without DB rows. Submitter still sees the success page either way. (h) Per-submission size/count enforcement happens at submit time, NOT upload time — operator can keep uploading individual files within per-field caps; the cap kicks in when they try to commit the whole submission. (i) `forms-public.js` uses vanilla JS / no framework / ~190 LOC — kept short and dependency-free per the brief's "no framework" stance. (j) Signature format (PNG/SVG) is read from the field config, not the request — the client-side `signature_pad` library outputs format based on explicit method call (`toDataURL("image/png")` vs `toSVG()`), not user input; this means a misbehaving client can't trick the worker into writing the wrong MIME. (k) `handleStaticAsset` returns null instead of a 404 when the path isn't recognized — the router decides whether to fall through to the next route or 404, which gives more flexibility for future asset additions. (l) The admin serve route gates on session BEFORE the path-traversal check — auth is the more restrictive gate; revealing path-traversal vs auth-failure differently would leak info. **Latent issues found.** (1) Wrangler glob mismatch — `static/*.js` doesn't match against the worker's source resolution. The `**/static/*.js` form does. CLAUDE.md updated with this gotcha so the next executor doesn't trip on it. (2) `signature_pad.min.js` is a UMD bundle (`!function(t,e){"object"==typeof exports&&"undefined"!=typeof module?module.exports=...`) — wrangler emits a "CommonJS module variable in ESM" warning even with the Text rule applied. Cosmetic only — the file is treated as text and never executed in the worker context; the warning is wrangler reading the file's first line before deciding to apply the Text rule, and persists in the dry-run output. (3) `file-type@^19.6.0` works in the Workers runtime with `nodejs_compat` flag (already set in wrangler.toml since Brief 89). Bundle grew +102 KiB from `file-type` + its `strtok3` / `token-types` deps. (4) `forms-public.js` writes status messages with `style.color="#666"` inline rather than reusing brand tokens — minor inconsistency with the rest of the form's styling but kept for two reasons: vanilla JS can't easily reach the SHELL_CSS variables, and the inline style is small enough that extracting to SHELL_CSS would add coupling without payoff. (5) The brief's draft Phase 7 example was buggy — it carried only `r2_key` forward in the parse step then enriched in submit. The actual implementation does the same thing and works correctly. (6) `signature_pad@4.2.0` has an `addEventListener` API that was added in v4.0.0 — earlier versions (v3.x) used a different event registration pattern; pinning to v4 ensures the `endStroke` event works as expected. **Files created.** `apps/forms-worker/src/limits.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/uploads/{file,signature,serve,static}.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/static-assets.d.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/static/signature-pad.min.js` (vendored), `apps/forms-worker/static/forms-public.js`. **Files modified.** `apps/forms-worker/package.json` (file-type ^19.6.0 dep), `apps/forms-worker/wrangler.toml` (`[[rules]] type = "Text"` block), `apps/forms-worker/src/index.ts` (4 new path matchers + 4 new imports + header docblock extended), `apps/forms-worker/src/db/forms.ts` (insertSubmissionFiles helper + SubmissionFileRowInsert interface), `apps/forms-worker/src/submit/index.ts` (R2 resolution pass + post-insert form_submission_files insert + HARD_LIMITS import), `apps/forms-worker/src/submit/parse.ts` (un-skip file/signature with hidden-input reads + comment update), `apps/forms-worker/src/render/shell.ts` (script tags injected in `<head>`), `packages/forms-schema/src/validators/payload.ts` (file + signature Zod schemas, replacing the Brief 91 nulls + comment update), `PRE_DEPLOY_FORMS.md` (Section 5 8 Brief-92 smoke tests including the SQL fixture extension), `CLAUDE.md` (forms-worker glossary Brief 92 paragraph), `BUILD_STATE.md` (this entry + Last-updated bump + prioritized work list row 92), `BRIEFS/INDEX.md` (Brief 92 row), `BRIEFS/brief-092-forms-file-signature-uploads.md` (Status → Completed; Outcome filled). **Validation.** `pnpm install` ran (file-type@19.6.0 resolved + 4 transitive deps); `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-worker typecheck` green; `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-worker exec wrangler deploy --dry-run` green at 975.33 KiB / 187.11 KiB gzip (bindings: FORMS_FILES R2, SUPABASE_URL var, TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY var); root `pnpm typecheck` 17/17 green. Smoke tests deferred to operator post-deploy per the brief's "Don't deploy automatically" line. | Claude Code |
| 2026-05-09 | **Brief 91 completed:** Forms feature — public submit path `POST /forms/api/submit/{slug}`. Second user-visible deliverable for the form-builder feature; combined with Brief 90 the form-builder feature now has end-to-end working text-only submissions for all three audiences. **Scope.** New `packages/forms-schema/src/validators/payload.ts` (`payloadValidatorFor(field)` returns per-field-type Zod value validators or null for the deferred types — file/signature/lookup wired in Briefs 92/93). New `apps/forms-worker/src/submit/` directory: `parse.ts` (multipart-form-data → typed payload keyed by field.key), `turnstile.ts` (Cloudflare siteverify wrapper, fail-soft when secret unbound, 8s `AbortSignal.timeout`), `success.ts` (Splash-branded thank-you page reusing brand tokens from Brief 90's shell, "Fill out another" relative-URL button per Brief 85), `index.ts` (`handleSubmit` route handler — the orchestration). `apps/forms-worker/src/db/forms.ts` extended with `insertSubmissionIdempotent` using direct PostgREST `fetch()` + `Prefer: return=representation,resolution=ignore-duplicates,?on_conflict=id`; on conflict (empty `[]` body or 409) falls back to a SELECT-by-id read so caller always gets the canonical `SubmissionRow`. Returns `{row, wasNew}` for observability — Brief 97's webhook fire can use `wasNew` to avoid double-firing on idempotent retries. Worker `Env` interface gains required `SUPABASE_ANON_KEY: string` (needed by `@splash/auth authenticate()` for `/auth/v1/user`; Brief 90 didn't need it because the render-time gate only inspected cookie presence). Submit route wired into router. **Audience model (planning Decision 8).** `public` → Turnstile verified when `form.turnstile_required = true`, anonymous submitter, captures `submitter_ip`. `internal` → `authenticate()` validates the cookie; on miss returns 401 `session_expired` JSON so Brief 90's user-facing JS can surface a "session expired" CTA without losing in-memory form state (Decision 8b). `link-only` → opportunistic session capture (operator submitting from inside admin), anonymous fallback (slug is the gate). All audiences: CSRF gate via `isOriginAllowed`; `pending_submission_id` UUID-regex validated; payload validation aggregated into `{error: "validation_failed", fields: {key: msg}}` 422 so user sees every problem in one round-trip. **Decisions made on operator's behalf.** (a) `@splash/auth` API mismatch — brief sample called `validateSession(env, cookieHeader)` but the actual export is `authenticate(request, env)` returning `{status, session?}`; used the actual API. (b) `jsonError(status, message)` is 2-arg, not the brief's 3-arg `(status, code, message)`; encoded structured error codes via prefix on the message (`session_expired: …`, `bad_origin`, `turnstile_failed: …`); validation errors use a custom `JSON.stringify` because `{error, fields}` is two-key. (c) PostgREST `Prefer: resolution=ignore-duplicates` over the brief's `@supabase/supabase-js` `.insert(..., {ignoreDuplicates: true})` — file already uses direct fetch (Brief 90 / 71 pattern) and the SDK isn't a forms-worker direct dep. (d) `SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` added as required `Env` field (typecheck-enforced); operator must `wrangler secret put SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` before internal-audience submits work. (e) Stale link-only session cookie → anonymous, NOT 401 (slug is the gate). (f) Validation aggregated rather than fail-fast for better UX. (g) Empty-optional payloads omitted from JSONB rather than stored as empty string (cleaner for Brief 96 admin UI). (h) `multi.required` defaults `min` to 1 when not explicitly set. (i) Form `audience !== "published"` returns 410 (Gone), not 404 — distinguishes never-published / archived from "doesn't exist". (j) Success page does NOT reuse `render/shell.ts` — different body shape, two distinct files cleaner. (k) `form.successMessage` defaults to "Thank you for your submission." when null. **Latent issues found.** (1) `SUPABASE_ANON_KEY` not bound on `splash-forms` today; Brief 89 didn't include it because Brief 90 didn't need it. PRE_DEPLOY_FORMS.md Section 2 now flags it as a pre-deploy operator step. (2) Bundle grew from Brief 90's 152.42 KiB / 27.04 KiB gzip to 872.94 KiB / 163.14 KiB gzip — 5.7× growth from `@splash/auth` + transitive `@supabase/supabase-js`. Unavoidable for internal-audience auth; well inside CF's 3 MiB compressed limit. Future claw-back would require inlining a minimal `getAuthContext` (forms-wide refactor, not Brief 91 scope). **Files created.** `packages/forms-schema/src/validators/payload.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/submit/{parse,turnstile,index,success}.ts`. **Files modified.** `packages/forms-schema/src/validators/index.ts` (re-exports payload alongside field-config), `apps/forms-worker/src/db/forms.ts` (insertSubmissionIdempotent + SubmissionRow + InsertSubmissionArgs), `apps/forms-worker/src/index.ts` (Env gains SUPABASE_ANON_KEY; submit route wired; header docblock extended), `apps/forms-worker/wrangler.toml` (SUPABASE_ANON_KEY documentation in comment block), `PRE_DEPLOY_FORMS.md` (Section 2 SUPABASE_ANON_KEY step + Section 5 8 Brief-91 smoke tests covering all three audiences, idempotency curl, validation_failed, bad_origin), `BUILD_STATE.md` (this entry + Last-updated bump + prioritized work list row 91), `BRIEFS/INDEX.md` (Brief 91 row), `BRIEFS/brief-091-forms-public-submit.md` (Status → Completed; Outcome filled). **Validation.** `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-schema typecheck` green; `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-worker typecheck` green; `pnpm --filter @splash/forms-worker exec wrangler deploy --dry-run` green (872.94 KiB / 163.14 KiB gzip — bindings: FORMS_FILES R2, SUPABASE_URL var, TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY var); root `pnpm typecheck` 17/17 packages green. Smoke tests deferred to operator post-deploy per the brief's "don't deploy automatically" line. | Claude Code |
| 2026-05-09 | **Brief 90 completed:** Forms feature — public render path (`GET /forms/{slug}`). First user-visible deliverable for the form-builder feature; the worker is no longer a 404 stub for public form URLs. **What landed.** (1) `packages/forms-schema/src/types.ts` Field union expansion: replaced the Brief 89 `export type Field = FieldBase;` placeholder with the full 16-interface discriminated union (HeadingField / ImageField / NameField / EmailField / PhoneField / ShortTextField / LongTextField / HiddenField / DropdownField / MultiField / DateField / TimeField / FileField / SignatureField / LocationField / LookupField). Added `LocationOption` interface used by the worker's pre-baked Location dropdown — separated from the LookupSource type so the public render path doesn't need to import the lookup registry just to type its option list. The Field discriminator is `type` literal — TypeScript catches forgotten branches in `render/fields/index.ts`'s switch at compile time. (2) `packages/forms-schema/src/validators/field-config.ts` (NEW): Zod schemas for all 16 field types (mirroring the TS interfaces) plus `fieldSchema = z.discriminatedUnion("type", [...all 16])` and `formSchemaSchema = z.object({ fields: z.array(fieldSchema) })`. Common base spread inline (`fieldBaseSchema`) since spreading a `z.object().shape` produces the same DX as inheritance without the runtime cost. `validators/index.ts` re-exports the new module replacing Brief 89's `export {};` stub. (3) `apps/forms-worker/src/db/forms.ts` (NEW): three helpers using direct PostgREST `fetch()` with `SUPABASE_SERVICE_KEY` headers — matches the Brief 71 `maintainx-users.ts` pattern, NOT the brief sample's `createClient` from `@supabase/supabase-js`. Rationale below under Decisions. `getFormBySlug` returns `FormMeta | null` (slug eq, single-row read). `getCurrentVersion` returns `FormVersion | null` (id eq + form_id eq, runs `formSchemaSchema.safeParse` on the JSONB `schema` column — that's the runtime boundary check that prevents a hand-edited row from breaking render). `getLocationOptionsFromPricingSimple` returns `LocationOption[]` (PostgREST `pricing` filter `in.(full,partial)`, ordered `location_code.asc,sort.asc`, JS-side de-dup on `location_code` since PostgREST `distinct` doesn't compose well with multi-column selects; 5000-row safety cap). (4) `apps/forms-worker/src/render/shell.ts` (NEW): `renderShell({form, bodyHtml, turnstileSiteKey?})` returns the `<!DOCTYPE>` + `<head>` (with Turnstile script tag included only when site key bound) + Splash navy header bar with white-script logo from `@splash/storage-r2 ASSETS.logoWhite` + `<main>` wrapper around the body. Inline `SHELL_CSS` ~70 lines: brand tokens (`--splash-navy`, `--splash-blue`, `--splash-cyan`, etc.), reset + base, header / form layout / field wrapper / 4 heading sizes / image (3 widths) / multi checkbox group / disabled-lookup / file + signature placeholder / submit button / Turnstile wrap. (5) `apps/forms-worker/src/render/index.ts` (NEW): `renderFormBody({form, version, locationOptions, pendingSubmissionId, turnstileSiteKey?, urlParams})` iterates `version.schema.fields`, dispatches each to its per-type render via `renderField(field, ctx)`, wraps in `<form action="/forms/api/submit/{slug}" method="post" enctype="multipart/form-data">` with hidden `pending_submission_id` (UUID generated server-side) + Turnstile widget (when audience=public + site key bound) + submit button. (6) `apps/forms-worker/src/render/util.ts` (NEW): `escapeHtml` / `fieldLabel` / `fieldHelp` shared helpers. (7) `apps/forms-worker/src/render/fields/` (NEW directory, 17 files): `index.ts` exhaustive `switch (field.type)` dispatcher; 16 per-type modules averaging ~15 lines each. `heading.ts` and `image.ts` produce no input element (display-only); `lookup.ts` renders `disabled` text input with placeholder "Select {keyFieldLabel} to populate" and `data-lookup-*` attributes carrying full source metadata for Brief 93 to wire client-side; `file.ts` and `signature.ts` render their inputs but Brief 92 wires upload behavior; `location.ts` pre-bakes `<option>`s from `ctx.locationOptions` (sourced from `pricing_simple` at render time, no client-side fetch). `multi.ts` uses `<fieldset>` + `<legend>` + checkbox group with shared `name` so FormData's `getAll(name)` returns the array. `hidden.ts` consumes `ctx.urlParams.get(field.defaultValueFromUrlParam)` to populate `?source=email_q2`-style inputs. (8) `apps/forms-worker/src/index.ts` (REPLACED): router with one real route `GET /forms/{slug}` → `handleFormRender`. Audience gating per planning Decision 8: `internal` checks for `sb-access-token` cookie presence and 302s to `/login?next=...` on miss (full session validation deferred to submit time per Decision 8b — render-time cookie-presence is a low-cost gate that avoids a Supabase round-trip per render); `public` includes the Turnstile widget when `TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY` is bound (verification deferred to submit time, Brief 91); `link-only` treats slug as gate. Status filter: `forms.status !== "published"` 404s (draft and archived forms are not publicly visible); missing `current_version_id` 404s. Location options fetched only when the schema actually contains a Location field (avoids an unnecessary pricing_simple query for forms that don't use it). Response headers: `Content-Type: text/html; charset=utf-8`, `Cache-Control: no-store` (every render reads fresh — re-publishing a form takes effect on the very next request), `X-Content-Type-Options: nosniff`, `Referrer-Policy: same-origin`. (9) `supabase/forms-test-data.sql` (NEW): three test forms (`test-public` / `test-internal` / `test-link-only-x4kp9q2m7nf3`) with representative field-type subsets. CTE-based inserts (`WITH form_row AS (INSERT...) ... UPDATE forms SET current_version_id = ...`) so the `forms.current_version_id` FK is satisfied at commit time despite the `DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED` clause. File header documents the DELETE statement for re-runs (slug UNIQUE means re-running fails on the second insert otherwise) and notes the zero-uuid `created_by`/`last_edited_by`/`published_by` placeholders are intentional (forms.created_by has no FK to auth.users). (10) `PRE_DEPLOY_FORMS.md` Section 5 expanded with 8 smoke-test entries: SQL fixture run, public/internal/link-only renders, the 302 to /login on missing cookie, the 404 on nonexistent slug, the 404 on POST submit (Brief 91 still pending), and the hidden pending_submission_id verification. (11) `CLAUDE.md` forms-worker glossary entry extended with the per-field-type module convention + 5-step recipe for adding a 17th type (new TS interface in types.ts, new Zod in field-config.ts, new render module, new dispatch case, new builder-side renderer in apps/web Brief 95) plus the audience-gating mechanics and the `formSchemaSchema.safeParse` runtime boundary check. **Decisions made on operator's behalf.** (a) `Cache-Control: no-store` chosen so re-publishing a form takes effect on the very next request — edge caching deferred to operator measurement per the brief's "no caching at v1" stance. (b) Heading text node is `field.text` (not `field.label`) so labels stay UI-only metadata while heading text is the rendered body — matches the brief's interface declaration. The label is still escaped and emitted as a `data-field-key` attribute for builder-side reference. (c) Default Location `displayFormat` is required by the schema (no default — operator picks per field at form-build time); the test fixtures use `"name"` as the simplest case. The fallback when `name_and_address` is selected but `address` is empty falls back to just the pretty name (defensive against incomplete pricing_simple rows). (d) Test forms' `created_by` / `last_edited_by` / `published_by` UUIDs stay as zero-uuid placeholders since `forms.created_by` has no FK to `auth.users` (loose reference per Brief 89 schema) and these are test-only rows — the brief flagged this as a question but the zero-uuid choice is consistent with Brief 89's same call. (e) Worker DB module uses direct PostgREST `fetch()` with the apikey + Bearer headers (matches `@splash/db-supabase/maintainx-users.ts` Brief 71 pattern) rather than the brief sample's `createClient({ url, service_key, ... })` from `@supabase/supabase-js`. Three reasons: (i) `@supabase/supabase-js` isn't a direct dep on forms-worker — it's only transitively pulled via `@splash/db-supabase`, so adding direct usage would require either a package.json update (more deps to maintain) or relying on the transitive (brittle); (ii) the existing monorepo workers all use direct fetch for service-key reads, so this keeps consistency; (iii) bundle size — supabase-js is 100+ KiB; raw fetch is 0 KiB. The brief's sample was a suggested shape, not a contract. (f) Multi-field rendered as `<fieldset>` + `<legend>` + checkbox group with shared `name` so FormData's `getAll(name)` returns the array; the brief's prose described it generically and HTML5's `required` on multi-checkboxes is unreliable across browsers. Submit-time validation (Brief 91) is the authoritative gate for `required` + `minSelected` + `maxSelected`. (g) Lookup field's `data-*` attributes carry full source metadata (`data-lookup-source-table` / `data-lookup-source-column` / `data-lookup-key-column` / `data-lookup-resolution-mode` / `data-lookup-null-behavior` plus `data-lookup-key-field` to identify the key field's id) so Brief 93's client-side wiring can read everything from the DOM without a second schema fetch. (h) Image field renders against placeholder URL `/forms/api/asset/{form_id}/{assetId}` — Brief 92's R2-backed asset serving will fulfill that route; Brief 90 image fields will 404 in the browser (broken-image icon) but the rest of the form renders fine. (i) Location field's display-format fallback collapses cleanly when address or site is missing (`name_and_address` becomes just `pretty` if address is empty; `site_number` becomes just `pretty` if site is empty) — defensive against incomplete pricing_simple rows. (j) `formSchemaSchema.safeParse` runs in `getCurrentVersion`, NOT in the route handler — keeps the boundary check colocated with the data read, so a future executor adding a Brief 96 admin-side preview that reuses `getCurrentVersion` automatically benefits from the same protection. **`@splash/auth` cookie helper confirmation.** `ACCESS_TOKEN_COOKIE` is the right export from `@splash/auth` (declared in `packages/auth/src/cookies.ts` L8 with value `"sb-access-token"`); imported and used in the audience gate. **Per-field-type module count.** Confirmed: 16 modules + dispatcher + util + shell + body = 19 files in `apps/forms-worker/src/render/`. No field type required a non-trivial divergence from the boilerplate — the 16 modules averaged ~15 lines. Multi was the slightly unusual case (fieldset/legend/checkbox group instead of single input) but stayed in the 30-line range. **Inline CSS size.** `SHELL_CSS` final byte count: ~3.2 KB (~70 LOC after minification of internal whitespace would shrink further). Well under the 10 KB threshold the brief flagged for "consider extracting now"; staying inline matches the fleet/signup posture and keeps TTFB fast. **Latent issues addressed.** (i) Brief 89's `validators/index.ts` `export {};` stub had to be REPLACED — the brief explicitly called this out and Brief 90 followed the guidance (replace with `export * from "./field-config.js";`). (ii) The `Field = FieldBase` placeholder in `types.ts` was REPLACED with the discriminated union — that's a load-bearing replacement, not an extension; verified no other files imported `Field` expecting the old shape. (iii) `forms-test-data.sql` had to use CTE-style inserts because the `forms.current_version_id` FK is `DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED` but a single statement can't satisfy the circular reference; the CTE chain inserts the form, inserts the version (which references form_id), then UPDATEs the form's `current_version_id` to the new version's id — all within one transaction. **Smoke results.** No manual deploy by executor — operator runs the SQL fixture and `wrangler deploy` themselves per the brief's "Don't deploy to Cloudflare automatically" line. Smoke testing deferred to operator post-deploy. **Validation.** See per-step results in the brief's Outcome section. **Files created:** `packages/forms-schema/src/validators/field-config.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/db/forms.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/render/shell.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/render/index.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/render/util.ts`, `apps/forms-worker/src/render/fields/index.ts`, plus 16 per-type render modules under `apps/forms-worker/src/render/fields/` (heading / image / name / email / phone / short-text / long-text / hidden / dropdown / multi / date / time / file / signature / location / lookup), `supabase/forms-test-data.sql`. **Files modified:** `packages/forms-schema/src/types.ts` (Field union expansion + `LocationOption` interface added + LookupKeyColumn import added), `packages/forms-schema/src/validators/index.ts` (re-export the new field-config module replacing the stub), `apps/forms-worker/src/index.ts` (router + handleFormRender replacing the 404 stub), `PRE_DEPLOY_FORMS.md` (Section 5 smoke-tests added), `CLAUDE.md` (forms-worker glossary extended with per-field-type module convention + 5-step recipe), `BUILD_STATE.md` (this entry + Last-updated bump + prioritized work list row 90), `BRIEFS/INDEX.md` (Brief 90 row added), `BRIEFS/brief-090-forms-public-render.md` (Status → Completed; Outcome filled). | Claude Code |

| 2026-05-09 | **Brief 87 completed:** Fleet detail page — editable `splash_notes` textarea at top of `/admin/fleet/[id]`. **Trigger.** Operator added a `splash_notes` text column to `fleet_submissions` 2026-05-09 for sales-follow-up annotations ("called, left voicemail, will follow up Friday", "qualified — needs demo", etc.). Brief 87 surfaces that column as an editable textarea at the top of the per-submission detail page. **Worker (`apps/fleet-inquiry-worker/src/admin.js`).** New `PATCH /admin/api/submissions/{id}` endpoint sharing the `authenticateAdmin` gate with the existing GETs (allowed: `session.role === "super_admin"` OR `session.dcRole === "admin"|"super_admin"`). Body parsed as JSON; `splash_notes` validated as string (400 on non-string), trimmed (so a stray newline doesn't get persisted), capped at 10000 chars (400 on over-cap). PATCH issued against PostgREST `fleet_submissions?id=eq.{id}` with `Prefer: return=representation` so the updated row comes back; empty representation array → 404 ("submission not found"); non-2xx → 500 with the same `console.error` posture as the GET handlers. New module-level constant `SPLASH_NOTES_MAX_LEN = 10_000` lives next to `ROW_LIMIT`/`CSV_SAFETY_CAP`/`DEFAULT_WINDOW_DAYS`. Router edits: OPTIONS preflight `Access-Control-Allow-Methods` extended to `"GET, PATCH, OPTIONS"`; top-level method gate widened from `!== "GET"` to `!== "GET" && !== "PATCH"`; per-path method validation re-applied inline so `PATCH /admin/api/submissions` (the list path) and `PATCH /admin/api/submissions.csv` still 405. Detail-path regex match dispatches PATCH → `handleUpdateSubmission`, otherwise → `handleGetSubmission`. `CSV_COLUMNS` array extended with `{ key: "splash_notes", label: "splash_notes" }` at the end of the inventory. File-top docblock updated from "Three routes" → "Four routes" with the PATCH endpoint described between the detail GET and the CSV export. **Apps/web detail page (`apps/web/app/admin/fleet/[id]/page.tsx`).** New `<section>` rendered above the existing key/value grid: title "Splash Notes" + helper copy + an `<ActionForm>`-wrapped `<textarea name="splash_notes" defaultValue={row.splash_notes ?? ""} rows={6} maxLength={10000}>` + a "Save Notes" submit button. `resetOnSuccess={false}` per the brief (operator may want to keep editing after a save). The action is `updateSplashNotesAction.bind(null, id)` so the page's id param is captured server-side; the textarea value travels through FormData. Existing key/value grid below the editor gained a "Splash notes" row that renders the value read-only via `<pre className="whitespace-pre-wrap font-sans text-sm text-splash-navy">` so multi-line operator notes preserve their newlines (the only column on the page that needs that treatment — every other column is single-line). **New file `apps/web/app/admin/fleet/[id]/actions.ts`.** Exports `updateSplashNotesAction(id, prevState, formData)` matching React 19's `useActionState` contract. Reads `formData.get("splash_notes")`, type-checks it's a string (defense-in-depth — the worker also validates), forwards via `updateFleetSubmissionNotes(id, notes)`, calls `revalidatePath('/admin/fleet/${id}')` on success, returns `ActionResult`. Try/catch wraps the worker call so any throw surfaces as `{ ok: false, error: <message> }` in `<ActionForm>`'s inline banner. `"use server"` directive at top. **`apps/web/app/admin/fleet/_lib/worker-fetch.ts`.** New `updateFleetSubmissionNotes(id: string, notes: string): Promise<void>` helper exported alongside the existing `getFleetSubmissions` / `getFleetSubmission` / `getFleetCsvUrl`. Same binding-first / URL-fallback pattern: try `getCloudflareContext({ async: true })` → `env.FLEET_INQUIRY_WORKER.fetch(req)` with `https://internal/admin/api/submissions/{id}` as the placeholder host (service bindings ignore host), Cookie + Origin forwarded, body `JSON.stringify({ splash_notes: notes })`, `Content-Type: application/json`. Catch falls through to a URL-based `fetch` against `workerUrl(path)` with the same headers/body. Throws on non-2xx with the worker's response text appended to the error message. `FleetSubmissionRow` interface gained `splash_notes: string | null` so the new key/value grid row + textarea `defaultValue` typecheck. **CLAUDE.md.** "Fleet inquiries admin" glossary entry rewritten — header bumped from "(Brief 83)" to "(Brief 83 / Brief 87)"; endpoint inventory grew from "Three new endpoints" to four (added the PATCH); CSV column inventory note ("now includes `splash_notes`") + detail-page editable-textarea sentence appended. Did NOT touch the existing line claiming filter column is `fleet_submissions.created_at` — that's stale post-Brief-86 (filter is on `submitted_at`) but it's outside Brief 87's scope and the QUEUE has Brief 88 next which may or may not touch it. **PRE_DEPLOY_FLEET.md.** Section 4.6 endpoint table grew a fourth row for the PATCH; section title bumped to "(Brief 83 / Brief 87)" with a sentence noting Brief 87 added the editor; CSRF paragraph updated to call out PATCH alongside the GET endpoints; smoke-test list grew step 7 covering the new editor (open detail page → textarea at top → save → inline banner → grid below renders new value → reload preserves → CSV download includes column). **Decisions on operator's behalf.** (a) Trim happens worker-side, not client-side — the textarea preserves what the operator typed visually; only the persisted DB value is trimmed. Avoids the surprising "I added a trailing newline and the textarea jumped" UX. (b) Read-only rendering of `splash_notes` in the key/value grid uses `<pre>` with `whitespace-pre-wrap` + `font-sans` — preserves multi-line formatting without monospace text styling that would clash with the rest of the grid. (c) Server action lives in a new `actions.ts` file rather than inline in `page.tsx` — Next 15 supports both, but the existing `/admin/damage/[id]` reference implementation uses a separate `actions.ts` and Brief 87 mirrors that for consistency (also keeps `"use server"` cleanly bounded — page.tsx has no client-island code so doesn't need to mix server-action + page rendering directives). (d) PATCH endpoint returns `{ ok: true, row: <updated_row> }` (not a bare `204 No Content`) — costs nothing and lets future callers consume the returned row without a follow-up GET. The current apps/web caller ignores the row body and relies on `router.refresh()` to re-fetch the detail. (e) Per-path method validation re-applied inline after the top-level method gate widened to PATCH — without it, `PATCH /admin/api/submissions` would have fallen through to `handleListSubmissions` which would have tried to issue a Supabase GET. Defense in depth. (f) Did NOT extend the OPTIONS preflight to add `Access-Control-Allow-Origin: *` for PATCH — service-binding callers don't issue preflights and the existing `*` covers the cross-origin GET case (browser console + curl). (g) Did NOT add `splash_notes` to `<input maxLength>` validation in the public fleet form — the column is admin-write only; the public submit handler doesn't accept user-supplied `splash_notes`. The 10000-char cap is enforced only at the admin PATCH boundary. **Latent issues / forward flags.** (i) v1 has no audit columns — `splash_notes_updated_at` / `splash_notes_updated_by` deferred per brief. (ii) No optimistic UI — `<ActionForm>`'s `useActionState` + `router.refresh()` pattern has visible latency between save click and updated grid render. Brief explicitly cites this as out of scope. (iii) Notes column not yet on the list page (`/admin/fleet`) — operators must click into a detail page to read or edit a note. Brief lists this as out of scope. (iv) No collision detection — concurrent edits silently overwrite. Brief 87 calls this acceptable until it actually causes a conflict. (v) The pre-existing CLAUDE.md "Filtering is on `fleet_submissions.created_at`" line is stale post-Brief-86 but left untouched (out of scope here). (vi) `splash_notes` is also writable from Supabase admin SQL or PostgREST directly with the service key — that's the Supabase service-key surface working as designed; only worker-routed writes get the auth gate + length cap. **Validation.** `pnpm --filter @splash/fleet-inquiry-worker typecheck` green (cache miss, fresh run); `pnpm --filter @splash/web typecheck` green (cache miss, fresh run); root `pnpm typecheck` 15/15 packages green (Turbo run after the per-package runs hit caches). `pnpm --filter @splash/web build` green — Next.js 15.5.15 compiled successfully, 14 routes generated, `/admin/fleet/[id]` 739 B / 106 kB First Load JS (grew ~575 B over Brief 86's 164 B from the new server-action + textarea client-island weight). `wrangler deploy --dry-run` for fleet-inquiry-worker green at 784.01 KiB / 149.16 KiB gzipped (small growth from the new handler — comfortably inside CF's 3 MiB compressed free-tier limit). | 2026-05-09 | **Brief 86 completed:** Fleet admin viewer — `created_at` → `submitted_at` column-reference fix on `apps/fleet-inquiry-worker/src/admin.js`. **What broke.** Brief 83 introduced `admin.js` filtering and ordering `fleet_submissions` rows on a `created_at` column that doesn't exist on the table; PostgREST returned 400 with `column "created_at" does not exist`, the worker non-2xx branch surfaced 500, apps/web `/admin/fleet` rendered "Failed to load submissions: 500" on every list / CSV / date-range call. Operator hit it on first load 2026-05-09 after Brief 83 deployed. **Why it landed.** Three converging signals nudged Brief 83's executor toward the wrong inference: (a) PostgreSQL convention of auto-`created_at` columns; (b) Brief 56's signups viewer (the pattern Brief 83 mirrored) reads `maxpass_signups` which has both a row-default `created_at` AND a JS-written `submitted_at`; (c) Brief 83's own `CSV_COLUMNS` list contained both `created_at` and `submitted_at` slots, suggesting the executor wasn't sure which was canonical and listed both defensively. The `fleet_submissions` table breaks the convention — it has only `submitted_at`, written explicitly by the JS submit handler at `src/index.js:368` (`submitted_at: new Date().toISOString()`); no DB-level default `created_at`, no Supabase auto-column. Brief 84's signups CSV executor probed the schema and got it right (kept `submitted_at`); Brief 83's didn't. **Fix.** One-file surgical swap in `admin.js`: three `created_at` filter/order sites in `handleListSubmissions` (gte / lte / `order=created_at.desc`) flipped to `submitted_at`, three identical sites in `handleCsvExport` flipped, plus pruning the bogus `{ key: "created_at", label: "created_at" }` entry from the `CSV_COLUMNS` array (now 21 columns instead of 22; `submitted_at` retained as the canonical timestamp slot). Two docblock comments updated in lockstep — the file-top "Filtering: server-side gte/lte on `fleet_submissions.created_at`" rewrite and the `CSV_COLUMNS` JSDoc "implicit `created_at`" claim. Final grep across `admin.js` for `created_at` returns zero matches per the brief's DoD. The `handleGetSubmission` detail handler (`/admin/api/submissions/{id}`) is unaffected — it filters on `id`, not the timestamp; was working at the SQL level even when the list/CSV paths were broken, but no operator could reach it because the list never loaded. **CLAUDE.md update.** The Brief 80 URL-by-analogy callout in the Work Orders glossary block (now at L771-L774) was extended into a four-sibling cluster: Briefs 76 / 80 / 62 / 86 each closed a bug caused by inferring a path / column / join key by analogy. New text explicitly cites `fleet_submissions.submitted_at` as the canonical example and contrasts it against `maxpass_signups.submitted_at` (Brief 84 grounded correctly on the same convention break) so future executors grep for the actual schema instead of assuming Postgres-default `created_at`. **Decisions on operator's behalf.** (a) The "implicit `created_at`" comment claims in the file-top docblock and the `CSV_COLUMNS` JSDoc were rewritten rather than dual-history-noted (e.g., "earlier draft assumed `created_at`...") — the DoD specifies zero `created_at` references in the file and that takes precedence over a footnote-style breadcrumb. The Brief 86 narrative captured here serves as the historical record. (b) Did NOT reorder the surviving `CSV_COLUMNS` list — `submitted_at` stays at slot 2 (where the deleted `created_at` had been at slot 2 → `submitted_at` at slot 3 collapses cleanly to `submitted_at` at slot 2), preserving column ordering for any operator who has already downloaded a CSV with the broken-or-not previous shape. (c) Did NOT add a `created_at` column to `fleet_submissions` via Supabase migration (out of scope per brief — `submitted_at` is the canonical timestamp the public form writes; adding a redundant column would be scope creep and introduce its own DB-level default-vs-app-write divergence). (d) Did NOT refactor `CSV_COLUMNS` to derive labels from row keys automatically (out of scope per brief; explicit list is fine for v1). **Latent issues / forward flags.** (a) The CLAUDE.md inference-warning callout has now grown to four siblings (URL paths, plural-vs-singular path segments, join keys, column names); future shapes of this same bug class (env var name inference, secret name inference, header name inference) would warrant promoting it from a glossary footnote to a top-of-file constraint section. Not in scope here. (b) The fleet `admin.js` test surface is empty — the brief did not add a unit test asserting the URL is correctly constructed; future polish could add a single-shot vitest checking that `parseDateRange` + the URL-construction logic emits `submitted_at=gte.<iso>` rather than `created_at=gte.<iso>`. (c) Smoke test deferred to operator post-deploy: open `/admin/fleet`, list populates with rows from last-30-days ordered most-recent-first; flip the date range and the list updates; CSV download works and the file's first row shows `submitted_at` as a column header (no `created_at` column); clicking a row's "View" link loads the detail page (was already working at the SQL level, just unreachable). **Validation.** `pnpm --filter @splash/fleet-inquiry-worker typecheck` green (single-package run); root `pnpm typecheck` 15/15 packages green (14 cache hits, only `@splash/fleet-inquiry-worker` cache-missed and re-ran). DoD's `pnpm --filter @splash/fleet-inquiry-worker build` is unrunnable — fleet-inquiry-worker has no `build` script in `package.json` (CF workers don't bundle ahead of `wrangler deploy`; same posture as the other workers per Brief 79's latent finding and Brief 85's reaffirmation). Future brief drafters: spec `typecheck` only for fleet validation, not `build`. **Out of scope** (per brief): Adding `created_at` via Supabase migration; refactoring CSV_COLUMNS to derive labels automatically; backfilling Brief 83's INDEX.md row to mention this fix (Brief 86's row documents the fix, the Brief 83 row stays as-was); worker-side schema introspection at build time. **Files modified:** `apps/fleet-inquiry-worker/src/admin.js` (three list-handler sites + three CSV-handler sites + `CSV_COLUMNS` entry deletion + two docblock rewrites), `CLAUDE.md` (Work Orders glossary block — Brief 80 URL-asymmetry warning extended into a four-sibling inference-bug cluster citing Brief 86 as the column-name sibling), `BUILD_STATE.md` (this entry + Last-updated bump from Brief 85's narrative to Brief 86's), `BRIEFS/INDEX.md` (Brief 86 status flipped from `Ready for Claude Code` to `Completed (2026-05-09)`), `BRIEFS/QUEUE.md` (Brief 86 line moved to completed-tombstone block), `BRIEFS/brief-086-fleet-admin-fix-submitted-at-column.md` (Status → Completed; Outcome filled in). **No files created.** | Claude Code |
| 2026-05-09 | **Brief 85 completed:** Fleet inquiry success modal — "Fill Again" button. Operator review 2026-05-09: success modal currently dead-ends with a checkmark + thank-you blurb + no call-to-action; operator had to close the tab or reload to file a second inquiry. **Phase 1 (markup):** added a new `<button type="button" class="btn-submit" id="fillAgainBtn" style="margin-top: 24px;">Fill Again</button>` inside the existing success modal at `apps/fleet-inquiry-worker/src/index.js` (after the `success-message` div). Reuses the form's existing `.btn-submit` class for visual consistency with the Submit Inquiry button; inline `margin-top: 24px;` separates it from the thank-you paragraph above. **Phase 2 (handler):** new IIFE-scope handler block placed immediately above the existing `submitBtn.addEventListener("click", ...)` registration — `var fillAgainBtn = document.getElementById("fillAgainBtn"); if (fillAgainBtn) { fillAgainBtn.addEventListener("click", function() { window.location.href = "/"; }); }`. Defensive null-guard so a future markup refactor that drops the button silently no-ops instead of throwing. **Why a relative URL.** `window.location.href = "/"` resolves relative to the current origin: on `splash-fleet-inquiry.joshua-copp.workers.dev/fleet` → workers.dev root, on `fleet.staging.splashcarwashes.info/fleet` → staging root, post-cutover on `fleet.splashcarwashes.info/fleet` → production root. All three serve the form at `/`. Zero per-environment string, zero cutover maintenance vs. an absolute URL approach that would need a code edit at every cutover. **Why `/` not `/fleet`.** Both route to the same form-render handler per the worker's router; `/` is shorter and is the canonical form URL fleet has been served from historically. **Sanity-check on form state reset.** Per Brief 81's audit the form state lives entirely in IIFE-scope JS variables (selectedLocation, selectedPackages, allPackagesMap, locationData, userCoords, detailingRequested, detailingLocation, turnstileToken, turnstileWidgetId) — no localStorage, no cookies. A full-page navigation to `/` reloads the worker-rendered HTML and resets all in-memory state to a blank picker; new inquiry starts fresh, no state bleeds across submissions. **Phase 3 (sweep).** Grep across `apps/fleet-inquiry-worker/src/index.js` for absolute `splashcarwashes.info` URLs and `joshua-copp.workers.dev` references in client-side code returned ZERO matches — relative URLs already in use everywhere on the navigation side. Server-side `fetch(...)` calls (Supabase REST endpoints via `env.SUPABASE_URL`, Google Maps Geocoding `https://maps.googleapis.com/...`, Turnstile siteverify) stay absolute as expected — those are env-var / external-service URLs, not navigation targets, and aren't affected by cutover. **Decisions made on operator's behalf:** (a) Placed the new handler block immediately above the existing `submitBtn` registration rather than near the bottom of the IIFE — keeps "modal-related JS" colocated with the submit handler that opens the modal in the first place, and makes the click-wiring grep-able (`addEventListener` cluster). (b) Reused the existing `.btn-submit` class for visual styling rather than introducing a new `.btn-secondary` or similar — matches the brief's spec ("reuses the existing `.btn-submit` class for visual consistency") and avoids CSS surface growth. (c) Inline `margin-top: 24px;` per the brief, rather than a dedicated `.fill-again-btn` selector in the worker's embedded `<style>` block — single-instance use, smallest surgical change. (d) Documented the relative-URL convention in CLAUDE.md's fleet-inquiry-worker glossary entry (paragraph appended to the Brief 83 sentence) as a generalizable pattern for any future client-side redirect in fleet, signup-worker, or any other public-form worker that has both staging and production hostnames — saves the next executor from re-discovering the same trade-off. **Latent issues / forward flags:** none surfaced. The brief explicitly notes "Fill Again" doesn't reset Turnstile widget state in-place — it reloads the page, which is simpler and gives the cleanest blank-state guarantee; in-place reset (preserve-some-UI-state-but-clear-form-fields) was considered and rejected per Brief 57's same reasoning. **Validation:** `pnpm --filter @splash/fleet-inquiry-worker typecheck` green; root `pnpm typecheck` 15/15 packages green (only `@splash/fleet-inquiry-worker` cache-missed, everything else replayed). DoD's `pnpm --filter @splash/fleet-inquiry-worker build` is unrunnable — fleet-inquiry-worker has no `build` script in `package.json` (CF workers don't bundle ahead of `wrangler deploy`; same posture as the other 4 workers per Brief 79's latent finding). Future brief drafters: spec `typecheck` only for fleet/workers validation, not `build`. **Out of scope** (per brief): Fill Again button on the legacy `broad-shape-38b8` worker (CLAUDE.md constraint #6 holds — only the monorepo-deployed worker is touched); Fill Again on damage claim form (its own conversation; signup already has it via Brief 57); in-place form reset without page navigation; Close button on the modal; CSS animation on Fill Again click. **Smoke test deferred to operator post-deploy:** open the form on whichever hostname is currently active (workers.dev or `fleet.staging.splashcarwashes.info`); complete a full inquiry — pick location, packages, fleet size, complete Turnstile; submit; success modal renders with the new Fill Again button visible below the thank-you blurb; click Fill Again; page navigates back to `/`; form is in initial blank state (no location selected, no packages, no fleet size, fresh Turnstile widget); URL bar still shows the same hostname (relative redirect didn't accidentally route off-domain). **Files modified:** `apps/fleet-inquiry-worker/src/index.js` (markup edit at the success modal block + new handler block in the client-side IIFE), `CLAUDE.md` (fleet-inquiry-worker glossary entry — appended Brief 85 sentence with the relative-URL convention guidance), `BUILD_STATE.md` (this entry + Last-updated bump), `BRIEFS/INDEX.md` (Brief 85 status flipped from `Ready for Claude Code` to `Completed (2026-05-09)`), `BRIEFS/brief-085-fleet-fill-again-button.md` (Status → Completed; Outcome filled in). **No files created.** | Claude Code |
| 2026-05-09 | **Brief 84 completed:** Signups admin viewer — date-range filter + CSV export. Retrofit `/admin/signups/{loc}` with the shared `<DateRangePicker>` and `<CsvExportButton>` Brief 83 introduced for `/admin/fleet`. **Worker** — `apps/signup-worker/src/handlers/admin-signups.ts` rewritten: `handleGetAdminLocationSignups` now accepts `from=YYYY-MM-DD&to=YYYY-MM-DD&limit=N` alongside the legacy Brief 56 `days=N` shape (precedence: explicit `from`+`to` → legacy `days` → default last-30-days; mixed half-defaults rejected with 400 to keep the contract unambiguous). New sibling handler `handleGetAdminLocationSignupsCsv` mounted at `GET /admin/api/locations/{loc}/signups.csv?from=&to=` — RFC 4180 quoting, worker-rendered CSV with `Content-Disposition: attachment; filename="signups-{loc}-{from}-to-{to}.csv"`, same `adminGate` + `userCanAccessLocation` check as the JSON endpoint, defense-in-depth `isOriginAllowed` (CSRF on GET — service-binding callers from apps/web pass; cross-origin browser tabs rejected with 403), 10000-row safety cap (416 on overflow). The CSV `select=*` captures every user-facing `maxpass_signups` column (`id`, `submitted_at`, `name`, `email`, `phone` raw, `phone_formatted`, `location_code/pretty`, `package/pretty`, `today_price`, `monthly_price`, `terms_text`, country/city/region, `user_agent`, `confirmation_token`). The JSON list response now exposes `from`/`to`/`limit` alongside the legacy `since` field; `days` is non-null only when the caller used `?days=N` (so the response can echo it back). **Filter column = `submitted_at`** (NOT `created_at` like fleet — `maxpass_signups` was indexed on `submitted_at` from day one; called out in CLAUDE.md so future briefs don't accidentally swap). New router branch in `apps/signup-worker/src/index.ts` for `/admin/api/locations/{loc}/signups.csv`. No new package deps (signup-worker already had `@splash/auth` / `@splash/db-supabase` / `@splash/http`). **Apps/web** — `apps/web/app/admin/pricing/_lib/worker-fetch.ts` (the shared signup-worker fetch helper) gained `SignupsParams` shape and `getSignupsCsvUrl()`; `getSignupsForLocation()` accepts either a `SignupsParams` object or the legacy bare `SignupDays` for back-compat (keeps any third-party caller working). Page `apps/web/app/admin/signups/[location]/page.tsx` rewritten: swapped the 1/7/30 `<Link>` row for `<DateRangePicker>` + added `<CsvExportButton>` next to it. Legacy `?days=N` URL bookmarks render an inline back-compat day-filter strip below the date picker so the URL parameter doesn't appear ignored — first time it's loaded the page calls the worker with `days=N`; selecting any new date range or clicking Apply moves the URL into the `from`/`to` shape and the legacy strip vanishes. **Decisions on operator's behalf:** (a) Filter column `submitted_at` chosen over `created_at` — fleet's CSV picked `created_at` because that's what the brief explicitly specified there; signups was indexed on `submitted_at` from Brief 56 forward and the existing handler queried it. Documented in CLAUDE.md and PRE_DEPLOY_SIGNUP.md. (b) Mixed half-default rejection — when only `from` OR only `to` is passed, returns 400 instead of silently filling in the other half. Avoids accidental "what date range did I actually query?" ambiguity in the operator's bookmark URLs. (c) Legacy `days=N` row preserved AS-IS — the inline back-compat strip renders only when an old URL bookmark passes `?days=N`, otherwise it's gone. Operators using the new picker never see it. (d) `getSignupsCsvUrl` returns a same-origin relative URL when `NEXT_PUBLIC_SIGNUP_WORKER_URL` is unset — same posture as `getFleetCsvUrl` from Brief 83. Works post-cutover when apps/web + signup-worker share a zone; requires the env var on staging today (apps/web on `staging.splashcarwashes.info`, signup-worker on workers.dev). (e) CSV column inventory chosen via `SELECT *` rather than an explicit allow-list — the brief asked for "every user-facing column"; an allow-list would silently drop columns added in future schema changes (the trade-off is the CSV header reorders if Postgres column order changes, which is rare). (f) `signups.csv` mounted as a separate dispatcher branch (third-segment string-match) rather than a `?format=csv` query param on the existing endpoint — keeps the 200 JSON / 200 text/csv content-type split clean and allows `<a download>` to bind to a stable URL. **Latent issues:** (i) Same `sb-access-token` cookie domain caveat as Brief 83 — works post-cutover when signup-worker is on the same zone as apps/web; on workers.dev today the browser-issued CSV download requires the cookie to be reachable from the workers.dev hostname, which the dashboard-worker's `Domain=splashcarwashes.info` cookie does not cover. The service-binding-routed JSON list works regardless. PRE_DEPLOY_SIGNUP.md smoke step 13 documents the cross-origin caveat. (ii) The legacy day-filter strip surfaces only when `?days=N` is present in URL params — operators who hit `/admin/signups/{loc}?from=…&to=…&days=7` (mixed shape from a copy-paste error) ignore the `days` param entirely (since `from`+`to` take precedence). Acceptable; `days` falls off the URL as soon as the operator submits the picker. (iii) No cross-location CSV (i.e., "all locations in March") — endpoint shape is location-scoped to match Brief 56's existing UX. Brief lists this as out of scope; future brief if operator wants a global export. **Validation:** root `pnpm typecheck` 15/15 green (3.9s with cache hits on most workers); `pnpm --filter @splash/signup-worker typecheck` green; `pnpm --filter @splash/web typecheck` green; `pnpm --filter @splash/web build` green — Next.js 15.5.15 compiled in 4.5s; all 14 routes generated, `/admin/signups/[location]` 920 B / 106 kB First Load JS (matches `/admin/fleet` exactly — same component set); no regressions. `pnpm --filter @splash/signup-worker exec wrangler deploy --dry-run` green at 777.63 KiB / 150.21 KiB gzip (modest growth absorbed inside the existing `admin-signups.ts` file; new CSV-rendering and date-parsing helpers add ~40 LOC, no new package weight). Signup-worker has no `build` script (per Brief 79's latent finding for monorepo workers) so wrangler dry-run is the build verification. **Files modified:** `apps/signup-worker/src/handlers/admin-signups.ts` (rewritten — new `parseDateRange`, `parseLimit`, `handleGetAdminLocationSignupsCsv`, expanded JSON response shape, new CSV column inventory), `apps/signup-worker/src/index.ts` (router branch + import + path-layout doc), `apps/web/app/admin/pricing/_lib/worker-fetch.ts` (new `SignupsParams`/`getSignupsCsvUrl` exports; `getSignupsForLocation` overload for back-compat), `apps/web/app/admin/signups/[location]/page.tsx` (rewritten — date-range picker + CSV button, legacy day-filter back-compat strip, parseLegacyDays helper), `CLAUDE.md` (Signup admin glossary entry rewritten with Brief 84 endpoint inventory), `PRE_DEPLOY_SIGNUP.md` (smoke-test steps 10-15: legacy `days` shape, new `from/to` shape, invalid-from rejection, CSV download, bad-origin CSRF, per-location scope rejection), `BUILD_STATE.md` (this entry + Last-updated bump), `BRIEFS/INDEX.md` (Brief 84 status flipped to `Completed (2026-05-09)`), `BRIEFS/QUEUE.md` (Brief 84 line moved to completed-tombstone block), `BRIEFS/brief-084-signups-viewer-date-range-and-csv.md` (Status → Completed; Outcome filled in). **No files created** — Brief 84 reuses Brief 83's `<DateRangePicker>` and `<CsvExportButton>` verbatim. **Operator follow-ups before usable:** (1) deploy `splash-signup-next` (push-to-GH if CF Builds is wired or `pnpm --filter @splash/signup-worker exec wrangler deploy`); (2) deploy apps/web; (3) optional — set `NEXT_PUBLIC_SIGNUP_WORKER_URL` in apps/web's CF Workers Builds env if running in cross-origin staging mode (skip post-cutover); (4) smoke per PRE_DEPLOY_SIGNUP.md steps 10-15. | Claude Code |

| 2026-05-09 | **Brief 79 completed:** Work Orders — rename "Preventive" tab label to "Preventative" + worker-side filter drops Preventive WOs more than 90 days past `dueDate`. **Two changes in one brief, both narrow.** (1) Worker-side `bucketByType` (`apps/workorders-worker/src/index.ts:359`) gains a 90-day overdue trim: new `PREVENTATIVE_MAX_OVERDUE_DAYS = 90` module-scope constant near `PRIORITY_NONE_RANK`; the loop now pins `nowMs = Date.now()` and `todayUtc = Math.floor(nowMs / 86_400_000)` before iterating so all WOs use the same reference, and inside the `wo.type === "PREVENTIVE"` branch it parses `wo.dueDate` (only when non-null and `Number.isFinite(Date.parse(...))`), day-floors it, and `continue`s past the bucket pushes when `todayUtc - dueUtc > 90`. NULL / malformed dueDate Preventive WOs are KEPT (operator can still see undated preventives; only the dated long tail trims). Reactive WOs are unaffected by the filter regardless of dueDate. The function's return type widened from `{ reactive, preventive }` to `{ reactive, preventive, droppedOverduePreventive }`; the dropped count threads into the existing `workorders-worker list:` log line so dashboards / log searches can track the filter's volume over time without an extra log emission. Day-floor math (`Math.floor(ms / 86_400_000)`) mirrors Brief 73's `<DueDatePill>` semantics so the worker-side filter never disagrees with the client-side pill on what counts as "today". Future threshold edits = single-constant change; if operator wants 60 or 120 days, edit `PREVENTATIVE_MAX_OVERDUE_DAYS` and ship. (2) Client-side tab label flip in `apps/web/app/workorders/_components/WorkOrdersTabsClient.tsx` — 4 surgical edits to user-facing display strings only: docblock L3 ("Reactive / Preventive tabbed view" → "Reactive / Preventative tabbed view"), docblock L15 ("Due column on the Preventive tab only" → "Preventative tab only"), L301 `<TabButton label="Preventive">` → `label="Preventative"`, L393 `BucketEmptyState`'s `tab === "reactive" ? "Reactive" : "Preventive"` → `: "Preventative"`. Plus one sibling docblock flip in `DueDatePill.tsx` L3 ("Preventive-tab due-date indicator" → "Preventative-tab due-date indicator"). **Internal-vs-display split (intentional):** state literal `"preventive"`, `TabKey = "reactive"\|"preventive"\|"new"`, `WorkOrdersGroup.preventive` field, URL search-param value `?tab=preventive`, MaintainX API references (`wo.type === "PREVENTIVE"`, `PREVENTIVE` enum literal in `bucketByType`), and `console.log` text all stay unchanged — the MaintainX upstream enum is `PREVENTIVE` (their spelling, their API contract), aligning Splash internal state to that enum keeps the code grep-able with MaintainX documentation, and lowercase `"preventive"` in URLs preserves back-compat with any operator-bookmarked tab URLs (`?tab=preventive&request_ok=N`). Operator's framing on 2026-05-09 was "preventive isn't a word"; both spellings are valid English (Merriam-Webster lists "preventative" as a variant) but operator preference rules on user-facing labels. **Out of scope (per brief):** renaming the internal state literal, renaming the response-shape field, adding a per-tab "show overdue" toggle, threshold-by-role/location, Reactive-side filtering (Reactive `dueDate` is auto-set to same-day by MaintainX so a 90-day filter would never fire). **Validation:** `pnpm --filter @splash/workorders-worker typecheck` green; root `pnpm typecheck` 14/14 green (3 cache misses: workorders-worker, web, plus the typecheck graph dependency); `pnpm --filter @splash/web build` green (Next 15.5.15, all 13 routes generate; `/workorders` 5.39 kB / 107 kB First Load JS — no observable delta vs Brief 78 baseline). **Latent finding:** `apps/workorders-worker/package.json` has no `build` script — `typecheck`, `dev`, `deploy`, `lint`, `clean` only; same posture as the other 4 workers (Cloudflare workers don't bundle ahead of `wrangler deploy`). The brief's DoD bullet `pnpm --filter @splash/workorders-worker build` is unrunnable in the current workspace shape (`pnpm --filter` returned "None of the selected packages has a 'build' script"); executor relied on typecheck + the root build for validation, which is the established posture across Briefs 70–78. Future brief drafters: spec `typecheck` only for worker validation, not `build`. **Smoke test deferred to operator post-deploy:** load `/workorders`, confirm tab label reads "Preventative" with the count badge, confirm a known stale Preventive WO (>90 days past due) is no longer visible, confirm NULL-dueDate Preventives still render with the em-dash in the Due column, confirm Reactive tab is structurally unchanged. | Claude Code |
